Ha kaba thoh ia kane ka Kitab, la trei ryngkat bad 
kawei pat ka Kitab ba la pynmih mynshuwa shaphang U 
KHASI. Shisien ba lasdang ban pyrkhat ia la ka jaitbynriew, 
la mih ruh ki jingpasiaw ba dei ban buh ba ki dak lieh dak 
long ia ki katto katne ki jingjia bad ki jingiathuhkbana kiba 
dei shaphang la ka hiraa bad shaphang la ka jong ka shnong, 
Ki jingiathuhkhana kiba rim ki long kiba ym lah ioh jingia- 
rap na kino kino ki jingthoh, la shu shim tang na ki jmgkyn- 
maw jong ki riewtymmen katba dang lah ban lap ia ki. Ki 
don arbynta ba la buh lang hangne: ka Hima Arphewlai 
Shnong bad ka Shnong Tyngnger. 

Kq Hima Arphewlai shnong . Ki nongpuie kin shrm 
katto katne ki kynrum kynram ha ka babud ia ki kyrtong 
kine ki shnong. Kane ka long namar u nongthoh um lah 
ban ioh ia ka jingpyntbikna kaba biang na ka bynta ka jing- 
kylla jong ki shnong ki thaw na kawei ka por sha kawei pat.

Kum ka nuksa yn pyni kumne : (!) Ka shnong Synnei ka 
long kaba la ju don ar Sandi: ka Synnei Nilang bad ka 
Synnei Pathein. Kine baroh ar ki la lalum lang ki pynlong 
noh tanguwei u Sandi bad kawei ka dorbar, naduh mynno 
ka snem ym lah shuh ban shem trai. Ia ka Shnong Nong- 
shluit ym imat ba la hicw sandi ia ka ha ki sngi shuwa ban 
wan ki Pharcng; ki briew ki long kiba leit shong shnong na 
Synnei. Ha ki jingthoh pud hima U Syiem Sohra, ka hima 
jong u ka iadei pud bad ka Ilaka Synnei ym bad ka Nong- 
shluit kaba khang hapdeng kine ki ar Ilaka* Kumta, imat 
ka shnong Nongshluit ka ioh ia ka jinglong Sirdarship na- 
duh ka por ba la synshar ki Phareng. (2) Ka shnat Tyn- 
rong ka don tang artylli ki shnong naduh ki por shuwa ka 
Sorkar Phareng: ka Tynrong bad ka Umtlang. Ha ki 
snem ka jingwan nyngkong ki Phareng, ka shnong Tynrong 
ka iapait lai bynta: k a Tynrong Rumnong kaba sah ryng- 
kat ha ka Hima Shala, ka Tynrong Mawrap kaba khlad 
sorkar Phareng bad ka Tynrong Nonglyngkien ka kyn- 
toit kaba leit wad sordar kyrpang la ujong na ka Sorkar 



Pbareng. (3) Ka imat ba ka shnong Mustoh ka dang wan’ 
buhai shnong ha ka jaka ba ka don mynta tang khyndiat 
snem, lada ym dei phew snem, shuwa ba ki Shala kin wan 
shong shnong ha ka jaka jong ki. Hapdeng katne ki kynrutn 
kynram, ia ka hixna ba synshar ki syiem dkhar ki khot hi’ 
ka Hima Arphewlai Shnong. Kumba la kdew ha kane 
ka Kitab, shuwa ban wan long ka Hima Arphewlai Shnong 
ki Ia don ka Synjuk lang (Confederacy) ki laiphewsan tylii 
ki sandi : 

(a) Ki Khathynriew shnong kiba kynthup ia ki shnong 
Mawphew, Sohro, Nongbah Mawmasi, Mawbyrsiew, 
Thangkasya, Kurbah, Mawshohksing, Nongpyrdi, Tyrkhum,

Rumnong. Synjit, Lumbyntait, Lumtyllongum, Sohkhatait 
Umblai bad Umeit = 16 shnong. 

(b) Ki San Shnong kiba kynthup ia ki shnong Tyrna, 
Nongriat, Nongkroh, Mynteng bad Ramdait = 5 shnong* 

(k) Ki Sawshnong kiba kynthup ia ki shnong Tyngnger, 
Lyngkhom, Dcwsaw bad Nongtrai — 4 shnong. 

(d) Ki shnong Mawdon, Mawkhan, Nonglait, Sinai 
Mawshynrut, Synnei Nilang, Synnei Pathein, Tynrong, Um- 
tlang, Nongbah, Nongwar = 10 shnong. 

Ha ka por ki Syiem dkhar, kanc ka Synjuk Sandi ka 
la kylla long ka Synjuk (Confederacy) Arphewlai Shnong 
kaba kynthup ia kme ki shnong ha ka banyngkong : Ki San 
Shnong; ki Saw shnong; ki 16 shnong ba la pynkylla noh 
sha ki Lai shnong, Mawphu, Thieddieng bad Umblai; ki

shuh hadien da ki shnong Mustoh bad Shala. La ka la 
don ka jing kylla ha kajingbun bad jingkhyndiat ki shnong, 
ka kyrteng Arphewlai Shnong ka sah kumjuh.

paitjmgpra jong ka hima ka bym shym lah syrdep shuh.

KA ’TIEN SYLLOK 

I Mr. KYNPHAM SINGH 
B.A. (Cal), B.Sc. Ag. Eng., (All), M.Sc., (I.S.U., U.S.A.) 


I Pastor T. Rodborne i dang shu lah pynmih sa kawei 
ka kot kaba i jer kyrteng “Ki Khanatang Na Ri War.” 
Kane ka kot ka don bun ki jingaijingtip kiba long 
kiba kordor bad kiba lah ban long ki jingmyntoi kiba khraw 
na ka bynta ki “Political' Scientists” bad ki “Historians’’. 
Ha kane ka pyrthei kylla-stet mynta ha ki rukoin im lang 
sah lang bad ha ki rukom bishar synshar hangne ha Ri 
Khasi, ki jinglum jong i ia ki rukom synshar bad bishar 
shnong shapoh Ri War kin iai sah kuin ki jinglum kiba 
myntoi bad dorikam. Kumjuh ruh ki jinglum jong i sha-

phang ki kara trei kam ktah, ki jingbat jingngeit ki briew, 
ki rukom poi-kha poi-kurim, ki kam tamsa, ka iasiat thong, 
ka tyor behmrad bad shoh dohkha, ki long ki bynta jong 
kajingim u Khasi kiba ngim kham iohi shuh. Ki jingbatai 
bniah jong i kin iarap shibun ia ki “research workers” ban 
ioh ‘'materials” ban reconstruct’’ ia ka ‘‘social” bad ‘‘cul- 
tural life” jong u Khasi Hyndai. 

I la tjioh khyndiat ia ka “Revival” jong ka snem 1905. 
Kane ka “Revival’’ kaba la nch shisnem kynthih ka la ktah 
ia ki bun bynta jong ka Ri Khasi bad ka long kawei na ki 
“landmark” ha ka History jong ka jait bynriew jojug ngi. 

Ki don ki Khanatang ba i thoh kiba pher na kiba ngi la 
ju iohsngew; ki don pat kiwei kiba iathuh da kumwei ia ki 
jingjia shaphang kiba ngi la ju tip da kumwei na kiwei pat 
ki khanatang-kum ka jingleit rung ki 16 shnong hapoh ka 
synshar ka Hima Sob ra; ka jingleit poi ki Shala sha Shala 
bad shaphang u Ganga Singh. Hynrei ym don jingartatien 



VI 


ba kine Id khanatang Tyngnger km iamp ia ki "research 
scholars” ban ioh pyadap bad pynbiang, pynryntih bad pyn- 
janai ia kijingthoh jong ki shaphang kanc ka bynta jong ka 
History ka Ri Khasi. 

Hynrei don ki bynta ha ka kot kiba bun ki nongpule kin 
xram iahap jingmut; kin shem ba ki bakla; lane, ba kim 
biang. Kine harum khyndiat na kita : 

I. Haba i Reverend i thoh shaphang ka kheifi kur

kheifi kha ki Nongphlang (page 74) i la shu ringksai jubor 
bad ki ‘‘conclusions” jong i ki la long “superficial” bad 
“sweeping” palat, kumne : 

(a) “Ka jingtlot ka nongrim ka jingkheinjait ki Nong- 
phlang ka long (i) ba ki shu shaniah ha ki jingiathuhkhana 
pateng ba la shu thaw (legends) ki bym sngewlah long shuh. 
Kum ka nuksa, lah ban jer kyrteng ia ki Jait Shrieh, Jait 
Tham, Jait Diengdoh bad kiwci.” 

Imat i Reverend i la shu shaniah tylli ha ki jingthoh 
jong u raynder uba kyrteng u P.R.T. Gurdon bad i kham 
shaniah ha kajingbatai jong u ban ia ki jingbatai bniah ki 
para Khasi, kum U Hormu Rai Diengdoh bad U Sib Charan 
Koy Jait Dkhar. Shaphang kawei na kine kijait, ngi tip 
shai ba ki Jait Shrieh kim pat ju ong ba ki mih na ka Shrieh, 
namar ba ki iadei shikur bad ki Marwein. Ku bri tynrai ki 
Marwein ka dei jjsha Hima Lyngngam Hyndai-Shikpoh ka 
leit phet sha Nonglait bad ki la ioh ia ka bri Nonglait na ki 
Sohshang kiba sa leit shong noli bad long bakhraw ha 
Hima Nongsohphoh. Ki Lyngdoh Nonglait, ki Marwein 
bad ki Shrieh ki ialong shi kur, 

(b) (2) “ba ki jingiashim jait ki la long pathar than bym 
lah shuh ban pyniadei kur haba dei khamtam ha kajuh ka 
kyrteng jait.” 

Kiba dang ngeit bad kiba dang bat skhem ia ka kheifi 
kur kheifi kha, ki tip hi haduh mynta mynne ba ki iadei shi 
kur bad kino kijait. Ki Dkhar Sawkpoh ki dei ki Sawian, 



vii

lei Ngapkynta, ki Phanbuh bad ki Wanlang. Ki don pat kr 
tnatjong ki kum ki Dkhar (Doloi Nartiang), ki Kharsyn- 
tiew, ki Kharphuli, ki Phankon etc. Kine ki Dkhar Sawkpoh 
kim ialong shikur bad ki Khar Dympep, Khar Dohling 
bad Khar Sohnoh (Ki lai jait kiba iadei kajuh ka kur) ne 
bad kiwei ki jait kiba sdang da ka Khar ha klimat, kum ki 
Kharkongngor, Kharshiing, ki Kharhujon bad ter ter. Toi 
shisha ym ju don Jait Lyngdoh ne Lyngskor. Kane ka long 
tang ka thoh wad nam kumba pynbud M L A ne M P 
hadien ka kyrteng. Ki Lyngdoh Mylliem ki dei ki 
Marbaniang, ki Lyngdoh Sohiong ki dei ki Thabah, ki 
Lyngdoh Nongkynrih ki dei ki Nongkynrih, ki Lyngdoh 
Nongbri bad ki Lyngdoh Kynshi rub ki dei ki Nonkynrih. 
U Lyngskor ha Sohra dei u Khongwir bad ha Nongkrem 
pat u Mylliemngap. Ki jait Syiem jong kano kano ka hima 
ki iatip biang biang ba ki iadei kur baa kino pat ki jait 
Syiem bad kino pat ki jait ha kiwei pat ki Hima.

duh ka snem 1892 bad ka la neh haduh ka snem 1897. Ka

Hima Shala.

IV. Shaphang ki Khubor Niam i la thoh : 

“Ki Sahep Missionary kiba la sliimti ia ka ki la pynsa- 
ngeh ia ka da kaba pynmih bujii da “Ka Pateng Khnstan” 
kaba la im da kata ka kyrteng haduh ka snem 1902. La

ki Editor, ka kyrteng rim ka sah wat haduh u bnai October 
1903.”

Kat kum na ki jingthoh ha kiwei pat ki kotkhubor bad 
fcat kumba paw na ki copy jong kitei ki kotkhubor hi, “Ka

bad “U Nongialam Khnstan’’ pat de dei ka kotkhubor u

Klimta!!*’ ha ka snem 1897 sa bud “U Nongialam Khristan” 
ha u July 1902. Ki iamih rangkat ryngkat haduh u October 
1903, iap noh ‘Ka Pateng Khnstan” irn sa tang “U Nongia- 
lam Khristan

i ha kaba turn bad kaba thoh bad ka long ka kot kaba kor- 
d or bad ka ban ai pngniyntoi sin bun, ia kiba bun. Sngewsih

KIT AB I 


I KA HIMA ARPHEWLA1 SHNONG HA RI WAR. 

Ki don ki Kitab History ba la ju tlioh ia ka Ri ne ia k» 
briew, da ki briew ba iapher kiba bishar ia ki jingjia na la 
ka liang jingiohi bad jingshem. Kme borabor icim long kiba 
iadei jmgmut lang nadong shadong. Ka History shaphang 
ka Ri Khasi jong ngi kam pat long satia kaba biang. Dang 
don shibun ka jaka ba dang dei ban tih ban pynlong ia ka 
ym tang ban shu pynsngewtynnat hynrei ba kan kham 
iajan bad kiba la long shisha. Ban leh ia kane, kaba bha

(d) Ki Seng 

Ka ktien ‘*SENG” ka la mih na Ri War. Ka jingmut 
jong ka ka long kaba eh ban batai, ym don Ktien kiba biang 
ban buh ba kin kit ia ka jingmut kaba tynrai jong ka. Ka la 
don ha ka dustur ba tynrai jong ka jinglong War. Ka dei ka 
dustnr kaba la don naduh hyndai ha kata ka hiraa kaba ki 
khot ka Hima Arphewlai Shnong. 

Ha ka jingtip jong kane ka aiom, naduh ba wan syn- 
shar ka Sorkar Pharengia kane ka ri, la tip ha ki kot ki sla 
Sorkar shaphang ki Santylli ki Seng: (1) Ki Sawseng, (2) Ki 
Laiseng, (3) Ki Hynriewphew Seng, (4) Ki Sawshnong bad 
(5) Ka Seng ka Snsap ka Pari. 

1. Ki Sawseng : — Ki nongialarn ba rangbah jong ka shnong 
Mustoh, haba ki ia wan buhai shnong nyngkong, ki la long 
u Khang bad u Sah-ei. Ki nongialarn jong ka shnong Shala 
pat ki la long u Khrang bad u Ben. Ha Shala bad Mustoh 
ki la fiiew burom katta katta ia kitei ki rangbah baroh saw- 
ngut. Hadien ka jingiap jong ki, ki nong-Shala ki la pynmih 
sawngut ki rangbah ban long ki khlieh nc rangbah nongpyn- 
iaid ia ka shnong bad ka Elaka. Kita ki la long u Kynshe 
uba na ka ling u Sah-ei, u Jirkha uba na ka ling u Khang, 
u Sting uba na ka iing u Ben, bad u Bor uba na ka ling u 
Khrang. Ka jinglong traishnong u Kynshe bad u Jirkha ka

la long na Mustoh, u Sting u la long uba na Dewsaw uba la 
leit poikha ha ka ling u Ben, baroh laingut ki la kylla trai- 
shnong Shala da ka jingleit poikha joag ki bad u Bor u dei u 
traishnong Shala. Kine baroh sawngut ki la long rangbah 
oongsynshar shnong shirta bad ka jaka shong jong ki ka la 
long ha Shala. Imat ka kam jong ki ka la long ar ban, ka- 
wefkura ki rangbah nongmihkhmat jong ka hiraa bad kawei 
pat kum ki ’Men Shnong jong ka shnong Shala. Ha Shala 
hi, yrn ju don ’Menshnong ne headmen; ki Wadadar nong- 
Shala ha ki snem kiba bud, ki la shim ia ka jaka ka jinglong 
^Mensbnong. Kinc sawngut ki la long ki khlieh jong ka hiraa 
bad jong u Sandi ka shnong Shala kiba nyngkong eh. Ka 
Elaka Shala ha kata ka por ka dang long ka Ri Raid baroh 
kawei, ym pat donRi Kynti. Ki khun ki ksiew jong kitei ki 
Ophisar ba nyngkong ki la tap ia ka shnong Shala baroh 
kawei. la ka khyndew baroh kaba hap hapoh ka Raid

Shala, la khmih bad syoshar kitei ki Ophisar ia kiba la khot

jinglong Wadadar, ba ka dei ban long tang na ki Waris

2. Ki Laiseng : Ka la don kawei ka shnong kaba hapdeng 
ki shnong Mustoh bad Mawlong kaba ki khot ka Laitmaw-

kjit umbuifi na la ka kmie kaba ia lah iap. U la khot 1 a ita 
i kbunlung ban bud ia u, hynrei im treh bad hadien u la

3. Ki Hynriewphew Seng. La don shisien uwei u briew na 
Thieddieng uba kyrteng u WeifL Une u la long uba riew-

XV. KA CONSTITUTION JONG U SANDI TYNGNGER 
(KI KULAR SHNONG U SANDI TYNGNGER) 

KA JINGLAMPHRANG. 

Ngi ki Tymmen, ki Rangbah bad u Paiabah jong U 
SANDI Tyngnger, mynta ka sngi da ka jingiamynjur lang 
ka Dorbar u Sand', ngi iakut ban kynticw thyramai mynta ia 
ki rukom pyniaid shnong, ki rul ki riti kibalaju long bad 
pyniaid palcng-la-pateng haduh kane ka sngi, Jem bad ia 
ki rukom pyniaid kiba ngi dang shna thyrnmai na ka bynta 
ka jingpyniaid belt ia ka shnong ka thaw. 

Namarkata, da ka jingiamynjur lang ngi la iasoi la ki 
kyrteng ha ki sla kiba khatduh jong ki jingthoh ba la pyn- 
dep ha kane ka sngi, In ngm ym mkylla shuh. 

Dated Tvngnger, H. June 

The 31st. July, 1928 Secretary.

LYNNONG IV 

Ki Dorbar Shnong 

1. Ki Tymmen kin long ki nongpyniaid ia ki kam dor- 
bar. Haba long dorbar yn tied da ka baje. 

2. Haba la tied shisien ka baje dei ban poi ha dorbar. 
Ha kaba arsien tied baje yn thoh baisngi ia uba pep, bad ha 
kaba laisien tied baje yn sdang ia ki kam dorbar. (Uba pep 
dorbar un hap kuna 4 anna). 

3. Khnang ba yn pyniaid ia ki dorbar ha ka rukom 
ba itynnat bad donakor, yn ym shah ia kano kano ka jing- 
kren kvlmar. Uno uno uba kwah ban kren un da pan na 
ka dorbar. 

4. Ba yn ym shah ia uno uno u ban mih ha bym pat 
wai ka dorbar khiem da pan. (Uba pynkheift ia kane yn 
hap kuna 4 anna). 

5. Haba buh ban long dorbar, ki Tymmen kin jari 
shai bad kumjuh ruh ha ki dorbar ba donkarn ban long 
khiem da buhia lypa. 

6. Ba haba buhia sawsngi shuwa ban long dorbar, 
pun jingkieng, shna lynti, uno uno u bym don ha shnong u 
dei ban hap baisngi; hynrei lada buhia duna ia ka sawsngi 
lah ban lait baisngi. 

7. Ha ki sngi shong dorbar, pun jingkieng, shna lynti,

bad kiwei kiwei, haba la £em iano ianobanleit $ha kiwei 
pat ki kam shnong ha ki jaka ba dang lah ban leit rwing

kata y a ym daai baisngi; hynrei lada jia ba leit sahmiet bym 
lah ban wan rwing, kata dei ban ioh ia la u baisngi kat ka 
dor ba la buh. 

8. Ba ia ki jingiakut jong ki dorbar baroh dei ban

tboh bad katto katnc ngut m ki paidbah kin so i la ki

LYNNONG V 
Ki Kara Shnong Kara Thaw. 

1. Ha ki kam trei paidbah jong ka shnong, ki Tymmen 
kin pyniaid ia u paidbah >

wan poi haba la fliew baisngi un hap kuna kumjuh kum 
uba pep.

na kaba iatrei lang u paidbah ym dei ban shah. (Ubapyr-

(k) Ki Tymmen kin ym trei jingtrei ha ki kam trei 
paidbah jong ka shnong; kin long ki nongpyniaid ia u paid- 
bah bad ia ka jingtrei. 

2. Uno uno uba pep ha ki kam trei jingtrei jong ka 
shnong, uba ong ba u sngewpang, u dei ban shong haling 
san sngi. 

3. Ka jingkheih “don” ia uba pep pun jingking kan 
long kumne*- 

(a) Na ka bynta ka jingpep ka kuna kan long 8 anna. 

(b) Na ka bynta u skong kan long 4 anna. 

(k) Na ka bynta ki jyrmi kan long 4 anna. 

4. Ki kot ki sla kiba dei jong ka shnong ka thaw ki 
dei ban don ha ka list, ha ka Bui jong u Sandi,- ia kiba la 
don lupa bad ia ki ban dang wan. 

5. Uho uno uba leit siewkuna ne baisngi ha ki Tym- 
men, ki Tymmen ki dei ban ai Rosit; bad uta uba ioh ia

U Dr. Homiwell Lyngdoh, ha ka Kitab jong U kaba 
kyrteng KA NIAM KHASI, ha ka jinglamphrang, sla li, 
u ong kumne: “Bad don ruh ki kpoh ha ri Khasi kiba dang 
kynmaw bha ia la ki jingiathuhkhana pateng ba ki wan kiew 
sha Lum Khasi na shaphang Syntengbad na shaphang Bhoi. 
Imat ka long ka jingmlien u Khasi, ha ka jingwan kiew jong 
u sha kine ki Lum Khasi, ba, haba u kynriah jaka ne u 
kynriah shnong, u rah artat ia ka kyrteng jong ka jaka ne 
ka shnong ba u iehnoh bad u jer kyrteng ia ka jaka ne ka 
shnong thymmai ba u shong da kata kajuh ka kyrteng.

ta ban iohi ia ka jingsdang bad ka jingkut ka lynti iaid jong 
u ha kine ki lum. Ngi shem mvnta ba don ka Sumer ha Ri 
Synteng, bad don ruh ka Sumer ha Ri Khasi; ba don ka

Mawpat, ka Umniuh, ka Sohbar bad ka Nongjri ha Ri Khasi 
bad ha Ri War”. Halorjong kane ka jingshisha ngin bud- 
dien ia ki ktien Tyngnger ne Tynger shano ka ialam. 

Ka don kawei ka jaka hapdeng Myliiem bad Laitlyng-

K. KA JINGPYNB1T KAM SHNONG
